## Outcome

704 inventory ids (RENDERING-0001 … RENDERING-0704): 560 normative items plus 144 defined terms. By kind: rule 246, term 144, test 87, mechanism 46, budget 41, table 41, interface 31, constant 26, algorithm 25, type 10, decision 5, number 2.

Per section (words): §8 31 909, §9 6 554, §11 9 320, §12 11 375. Findings: 5 internal conflicts, 2 cross-part conflicts, 3 broken references (0 unresolved by script), 9 wiring gaps, 4 edges, 4 term defects; 17 cross-part restatements and 16 internal duplications (principle 1); 6 elegance and 10 narration candidates (principles 5 and 9). One g item names this unit and is absent here: item 112 (a) (F-MISSING for §8 and §9). 83 f items (not yet applied) touch the unit.

**Ownership.** `owner` is the fact's canonical home: `rendering` (this unit) unless the item restates a fact whose home is another base part (model §2, kernel §3, pipeline §4/§10/PART II-III, determinism §5-§7, scope §0/§1/§13-§17) or a volume. `subject` names the stage a base-owned hook row serves when it is not this unit's own: V01 neighbourhoods, V02 buildings, V03 interiors, V04 underground, V05 sky, V06 evolution (with §4.11's grow path), V08 SettlementForge, V09 application, V10 clerk, V12 calibration, or a pipeline section.

**Wiring convention.** `reads` = what the item depends on or is produced by; `used by` = what reads, calls, renders, exports or tests it. In-unit ends are inventory ids; outside ends are section and name. `outside_mentions` (JSON only) counts, per base section outside the unit and per volume, the occurrences of the item's identifier-like keys - found by script, for the wiring graph and the seam checkers.

**Evidence.** RIC-01 to RIC-05, RWG-01 to RWG-09, RXC-01, RXC-02 and RBR-01 to RBR-03 are CONFIRMED - each by reading both texts and, for the absence claims, by grep over the base and the twelve volumes (md5 01fea21f); RIC-03 by arithmetic. The REDGE and RTD items are readings of the unit's text (PLAUSIBLE until the canon rules). Every other numeric statement of the unit was recomputed and holds (byte bounds of the four tables, the canvas worked cases, §12.3's and §12.5's laws and knees, §12.8's lines, §12.9's ratios). Nothing here is a ruling.

| RENDERING-0057 | mechanism | §8.2 | Random style (N-26): each role L* within +/-12 of the family shipped value, chroma [0,34] in ink and [0,60] otherwise, any hue, road = paper kept where tied; a style-time draw from stream style.random (§6.1) written into the setStyle event as row operations so replay never re-draws. | L9742 | rendering | §6.1 style.random stream | §7.1 setStyle event; RENDERING-0058 |
| RENDERING-0058 | algorithm | §8.2 | Bounded repair: take the first failing pair in matrix row order; raise lighter role L* by 1 (up to 97) or, if at 97, lower darker L* by 1 (down to 3); re-check every pair; stop when all pass or after 100 steps; must pass after sRGB conversion; failed draw redrawn (attempts 0-2); after three failures the family shipped palette is used and the report says so; <= 300 steps (J-R30). | L9745 | rendering | RENDERING-0055 | RENDERING-0057; report |
| RENDERING-0059 | number | §8.2 | Measured on 2 000 seeded draws per family (amendc_rendering_palette.out): 9-27 % pass as drawn, 96-97 % after the first repair (median 4-12 steps, p95 16-39), 100 % within three attempts; fallback does not fire. | L9749 | rendering |  | RENDERING-0060 |
| RENDERING-0060 | test | §8.2 | Confirmation at M3b (random styles ship): shipped-palette fallback <= 2 % of 10 000 draws per family; pre-decided fallback: L* window narrows to +/-8 and the run repeats. | L9751 | rendering | RENDERING-0058 | §16 M3b; §13 |

Sampling: Bridson with k = 50 candidates, all evaluated, in candidate annulus [r0, (1+a) r0] on the torus, a = poisson.annulus = 0.1 (provenance ref:sampler annulus of MFCG, NG 1.2.2, VG 1.6.9, Dwellings (code), P14; item 53; textbook a = 1 the alternative and the value the receipts used); accepted points rounded to integer cm, spacing test exact integer compare of squared toroidal distance with r0^2; first point (int(0,6399), int(0,6399)); active point int(0, n-1) over the insertion-ordered active list, retired replaced by the last; candidate at radius r0 x (1 + a x float01) along angle 2pi x float01 (dmath cos, sin, §6.5), tried in draw order. | RENDERING-0354 | algorithm | §8.10 | Ranking per tile: each point's energy = sum over other points in point-index order of exp(-d^2/(2 sigma^2)) (d toroidal cm, sigma = r0, dmath.exp); the highest-energy point (tie: lower index) removed and given the highest free rank (n-1 first); remaining points subtract the removed point's term in point-index order (removal update: ~1.5 n^2 exponentials, O(n^2), never a re-sum per removal O(n^3): ~53 ms vs ~1.8 ms per tile at 161 points; ~2.2 ms at 178 points); a needed square root is Math.sqrt (exact by spec, §6.5 (2)); crown library shapes use dmath likewise so only sprite rasterisation is engine-specific; a class band and four tiles build in ~19.5 ms at the textbook annulus and ~24 ms at the shipped one (PLAUSIBLE until built); the annulus changes packing density, never construction, seam band or ranking (J-R11, J-R48, J-R50, J-R55; J-A53.81). | L10962 | rendering | §6.5 (2) | RENDERING-0350 |
